,[cmpCampaignIds],[cmpCampaignNames] /*RZ14010518- Add cmpCampaignIds*/  
,[cmpSuppUserId],[cmpSuppUser],[CmpScores]/*RZ14021111- Add filter cmpSuppUser and cmpKind and score*/ 
,[prjcmpPresentationId],[prjcmpPresentation],[prjcmpCampaignIds],[prjcmpCampaign] /*RZ14010725- Add Campaign and Presentation*/
		 
,CAST(ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY CompanyID)AS BIGINT) [RowNumber] /*RZ14000911- Add rowNumber*/ 
FROM ( 
'
+
@Query
+
')t Order by CompanyID'
)
--PRINT ( @Query)
/*RZ970828 NVARCHAR TO VARCHAR*/
DECLARE @Res VARCHAR(max)
DELETE CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE UserId = @UserId
And TypeId = 1
/**/
/**/
